## 3. RESULTS

## 3.1. The limit tracks repetition, not length

Fi<sub>g</sub>ure 1(a) is the central test. ## 3.2. The deficit is graded in the period

S<sub>o</sub> f<sub>ar, repe</sub>titi<sub>on an</sub>d <sub>per</sub>i<sub>o</sub>di<sub>c</sub>it<sub>y vary</sub> t<sub>oge</sub>th<sub>er; a secon</sub>d l<sub>a</sub>dd<sub>er</sub> h<sub>o</sub>ld<sub>s carr</sub>i<sub>er, wor</sub>d <sub>coun</sub>t <sub>an</sub>d <sub>reques</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>coun</sub>t fi<sub>xe</sub>d <sub>an</sub>d <sub>var</sub>i<sub>es</sub> <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e per</sub>i<sub>o</sub>d <sub>�.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>it i<sub>s gra</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d<sub>, no</sub>t <sub>a s</sub>t<sub>ep: exac</sub>t <sub>ra</sub>t<sub>es</sub> rise 10.4, 47.9, 73.6, 93.1% at <sub>�</sub>=1, 2, 4, 8, monotone in all four check<sub>p</sub>oints measured (Table 5; Fi<sub>g</sub>. 1b).

The decisive step is �=2: no token is ever adjacent to itself, <sub>y</sub>et half the deficit remains (0.547 on the strictest of five scorin<sub>g</sub> rules, 0.69–0.86 on the others). Nor is it a <sub>p</sub>ower-of-two artifact: at �=24 the odd ste<sub>p �</sub>=3 (62.3%) and non-<sub>p</sub>ower $p { = } 6 \left( 9 0 . 0 \% \right)$ l<sub>an</sub>d b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>e</sub>i<sub>r ne</sub>i<sub>g</sub>hb<sub>ours, as pre-reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ere</sub>d<sub>.</sub> A <sub>comma,</sub> f<sub>u</sub>ll <sub>s</sub>t<sub>op</sub> <sub>or</sub> “<sub>an</sub>d” b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> <sub>repe</sub>titi<sub>ons</sub> <sub>recovers</sub> <sub>none</sub> of it (�=465): the copies are segmented, just miscounted.

Whether the ordering of a period-� text or its restricted vocabulary carries the efect is unresolved. ## B. SCORING

Two flags. As built, Count A is Whisper large-v3 [14]; the jud<sub>g</sub>e audit (Section C) showed its autore<sub>g</sub>ressive decoder <sub>sys</sub>t<sub>ema</sub>ti<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y un</sub>d<sub>ercoun</sub>t<sub>s correc</sub>t <sub>per</sub>i<sub>o</sub>di<sub>c speec</sub>h<sub>, so</sub> th<sub>e</sub> main text uses a CTC judge scored by relative count error, <sub>an</sub>d Whi<sub>sper-</sub>d<sub>er</sub>i<sub>ve</sub>d <sub>num</sub>b<sub>ers</sub> <sub>are</sub> fl<sub>agge</sub>d <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> th<sub>ey</sub> <sub>recur.</sub> A judge swap changes only the transcript source: Count B is recalibrated per judge, and every transcript-independent piece <sub>surv</sub>i<sub>ves.</sub>

Three metrics. Count A counts exact whole-token target matches in the normalised transcri<sub>p</sub>t (lower-cased, <sub>p</sub>unctuation stripped, numerals expanded to number words). Count B in-<sub>ver</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>per-mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>,</sub> <sub>per-</sub>f<sub>am</sub>il<sub>y,</sub> <sub>per-</sub>t<sub>emp</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> li<sub>near</sub> fit <sub>o</sub>f d<sub>ura</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>on</sub> �<sub>, ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y on a</sub> t<sub>rus</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d l<sub>ow-</sub>� <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>me</sub> $( k \leq 3 ,$ <sub>,</sub> C<sub>ou</sub>nt A exact, duration > 0.2 s) fixed before seein<sub>g</sub> hi<sub>g</sub>h-� behaviour. An item is correct only if Count A exactly equals the expected count and the duration ratio against the fit lies in [0.70, 1.45].

Exclusions. Audio that is empty or degenerate (duration < 0<sub>.</sub>25 <sub>s,</sub> $\mathrm { R M S } < 1 0 ^ { - 3 }$ <sub>, spec</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>l fl<sub>a</sub>t<sub>ness</sub> $> 0 . 3 5$ , <sup>or an em</sup>p<sup>t</sup>y transcri<sub>p</sub>t) carries no count and is re<sub>p</sub>orted as its own rate, checked before an<sub>y</sub> transcri<sub>p</sub>t-based label. At |Count $\mathbf { A } -$ Count B| > max(1, 0.15·expected count) the item is a fla<sub>gg</sub>ed di<sub>sagreemen</sub>t<sub>, never a syn</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>se</sub>d <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er.</sub>

## C. THE JUDGE IS PART OF THE EXPERIMENT

## H. THE PERIOD LADDER IN FULL

E<sub>xac</sub>t <sub>ren</sub>d<sub>er</sub>i<sub>ngs</sub> <sub>r</sub>i<sub>se</sub> <sub>mono</sub>t<sub>on</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> <sub>w</sub>ith <sub>per</sub>i<sub>o</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> <sub>every</sub> <sub>c</sub>h<sub>ec</sub>k<sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>, an</sub>d h<sub>a</sub>lf th<sub>e</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>it <sub>surv</sub>i<sub>ves a</sub>t <sub>per</sub>i<sub>o</sub>d 2<sub>, w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> no token is adjacent to itself.
